# ops cleanup report

Phase 8; base `ea358c52`.

The original OPS folder mixed live Fleet discovery, enrollment, canonical host/telemetry/workload stores, backup and maintenance planning with old JSON writers and unwired HA/execution prototypes. Production application Fleet runtime → OpsToolService → StatePlaneFleetRegistry, SQLiteTelemetryHistory, desired/workload/receipt/journal stores. Background discovery uses scoped observed evidence and authenticated enrollment, never synthetic trust.

| Original file | Decision | Responsibility and usage evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `__init__.py` | KEEP | PKG-OPS fleet telemetry, lifecycle, orchestration and failover primitives. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `activity.py` | KEEP | Host foreground-activity evidence and durable operator overrides. Imported by `application/background_runtime.py`, `ops/capability.py`, `ops/placement.py`, `ui/settings_window.py`, `ui/tray_agent.py`. |
| `agent_discovery.py` | KEEP | mTLS Fleet-agent discovery against explicitly approved candidate endpoints. Imported by `application/bootstrap.py`, `ops/discovery_canary.py`. |
| `approval.py` | MERGE | Exact Fleet removal approval lives with FleetRegistry, its only authority consumer; state_registry imports that canonical type. |
| `backup.py` | KEEP | Encrypted backup/restore engine for canonical Sofía production state. Imported by `ops/backup_cli.py`. |
| `backup_cli.py` | KEEP | Operator CLI for encrypted Sofía backup, verification and restore rehearsal. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `bootstrap.py` | KEEP | Bounded Fleet agent bootstrap planning and execution. Imported by `application/fleet_runtime.py`, `ops/discovery.py`, `ops/windows_bootstrap.py`, `ops/windows_rekey_bootstrap.py`. |
| `capability.py` | KEEP | Read-only cognitive surface for OPS fleet state, telemetry and planning. Imported by `application/fleet_runtime.py`, `cognition/fleet_engine.py`, `composition/engines.py`, `composition/root.py`, `runtime/runtime.py`, `ui/tray_agent.py`. |
| `desired.py` | KEEP | Desired-state and drift reporting for fleet reconciliation. Imported by `ops/capability.py`, `ops/desired_store.py`, `ops/repair_plan.py`. |
| `desired_store.py` | KEEP | Durable desired Fleet state for reconciliation. Imported by `ops/capability.py`. |
| `discovery.py` | KEEP | Bounded Fleet discovery evidence and candidate registration. Imported by `application/background_runtime.py`, `application/fleet_runtime.py`, `ops/agent_discovery.py`, `ops/discovery_canary.py`. |
| `discovery_canary.py` | KEEP | Read-only supervised mTLS discovery canary for one approved Fleet target. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `durable_lease.py` | DELETE | JSON lease writer is used only by acceptance fossils, not composed production fencing. |
| `enrollment.py` | KEEP | Authenticated-evidence bridge from NET enrollment into OPS fleet trust. Imported by `ops/capability.py`, `ops/discovery.py`. |
| `failover.py` | DELETE | Synthetic promotion guard/coordinator have no runtime caller; no production cross-host consensus claim is preserved. |
| `failure_matrix.py` | DELETE | Synthetic recovery disposition matrix has no registered or operator consumer and duplicates host/workload states; only own tests and exports. |
| `fleet.py` | KEEP | Tracked asset. Imported by `ops/desired.py`, `ops/discovery_canary.py`, `ops/enrollment.py`, `ops/failover.py`, `ops/maintenance.py`, `ops/persistence.py`, `ops/state_registry.py`. |
| `history.py` | KEEP, delete old writer | SQLiteTelemetryHistory is the sole durable history owner; old JSONL is parsed only by its verified migration. |
| `lease.py` | DELETE | In-memory workload fence prototype has no production consumer; live local runtime lease owner is RUN. |
| `local_telemetry.py` | KEEP | Local normalized telemetry collection for Fleet agents. Imported by `distributed/agent_tools.py`. |
| `machine_bridge.py` | DELETE | Unused observation/telemetry bridge is replaced by production authenticated discovery and local agent telemetry. |
| `maintenance.py` | KEEP | Typed fleet maintenance requests. No arbitrary shell or argv surface exists here. Imported by `ops/reconcile.py`, `ops/remote.py`. |
| `matrix.py` | KEEP | OPS contribution to the message matrix. Imported by `cognition/matrix/defaults.py`. |
| `migration.py` | MERGE + DELETE | Live read-only MigrationPlan/Stage move into workload.py; test-only MigrationCoordinator.advance execution-state prototype is retired. |
| `model.py` | KEEP, delete duplicate enum | Live host/telemetry/workload contracts remain; unused WorkloadState duplicated WorkloadPhase without a consumer. |
| `orchestrator.py` | DELETE | Test-backend migration executor is never composed; live migration capability returns a read-only plan. |
| `persistence.py` | DELETE | Retire JsonFleetRegistry writer and SMB replace helper; migration decodes old JSON directly into canonical host models. |
| `placement.py` | KEEP | Tracked asset. Imported by `ops/capability.py`. |
| `reconcile.py` | KEEP | Durable, verified Fleet maintenance reconciliation. Imported by `ops/capability.py`. |
| `reconciliation_journal.py` | KEEP | Durable dedupe journal for Fleet reconciliation observations. Imported by `application/fleet_runtime.py`, `ops/capability.py`. |
| `recovery.py` | KEEP, delete prototype classes | Keep production backup evidence/objectives/recovery guard; unused UpdatePlanner/UpdateRing/HostUpdateAssignment have no CLI/runtime caller. |
| `remote.py` | DELETE | Maintenance-to-remote-operation adapter has no production caller; typed distributed remote control remains live. |
| `repair_plan.py` | KEEP | Conservative repair proposals from reviewed Fleet drift. Imported by `ops/capability.py`, `ops/reconciliation_journal.py`. |
| `state_registry.py` | KEEP, repair migration | Canonical Fleet host owner resumes partial imports, preserves later canonical rows, detects conflicting partial imports, verifies and retires legacy input. |
| `telemetry.py` | DELETE | Snapshot normalizer has no production caller; local telemetry/discovery supply real observations. |
| `windows_bootstrap.py` | KEEP | Operator-approved Windows Fleet bootstrap for one explicitly named host. Imported by `ops/windows_rekey_bootstrap.py`. |
| `windows_rekey_bootstrap.py` | KEEP | Strict-X.509 Windows Fleet rekey/bootstrap canary. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `workload.py` | MERGE | Cohesive workload instance/placement/migration-plan value contracts; no synthetic execution coordinator remains. |
| `workload_store.py` | KEEP | Durable observed Fleet workload-instance state. Imported by `ops/capability.py`. |

Retired nine modules, merged two tiny modules into their actual owners and removed uncalled update-ring classes/duplicate workload state. Package exports/imports were repaired. Exclusive prototype tests were deleted or narrowed; Fleet round-trip/removal/node-binding tests now use StatePlaneFleetRegistry and SQLite, telemetry tests use SQLite and raw legacy fixtures. Four migration regressions cover partial-write resume, preservation of later canonical observations, conflicting partial input retention and cached/durable rollback on failed transition. Legacy source files retire only after verification. A missing test import and invalid candidate-refinement fixture caught by the first gate were corrected before the final gates. Production migration remains a proposal; no execution or HA was fabricated.

## Validation and remaining scope

Compile passed. OPS/application Fleet/RUN/tool/cross-package gate 274 passed. Full suite 3,388 passed, 7 failed, 2 skipped (63.99s), only the diagnosed unavailable Ollama and filesystem/config platform cases. Searches for retired imports/classes returned no source/test matches. Diff whitespace check passed. Test-count reduction comes from deliberate fossil retirement, not skipped checks.

StatePlaneFleetRegistry owns durable Fleet hosts; SQLiteTelemetryHistory owns telemetry history. WorkloadInstanceStore, DesiredFleetStateStore and reconciliation journal/maintenance receipts own separate observed, desired and proposed/receipt stages. JSON is migration input only. The live RUN lease is retained; cross-host fencing/failover remains unimplemented rather than a second authority source.

Checkpoint: `git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/development/ops-cleanup-report.md`.

## Definition, import and reference inventory

Reference candidates below are lexical evidence, not a resolved Python call graph. Each method is listed; receiver identity, callbacks, package exports and module-local calls require inspection. Production paths and intentional offline APIs are identified above. Existing state rows/files belonging to retired prototypes are not deleted by source cleanup.
